- **defra-pii-redaction**: Intercepts all LLM interactions — prompts, tool inputs, and tool outputs — and redacts UK PII before data reaches or leaves the model, using a configurable Python redaction script. ([DEFRA/defra-ai-plugins](https://github.com/DEFRA/defra-ai-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/defra-pii-redaction))
- **defra-shared**: Cross-cutting Defra standards as installable skills and guardrail hooks: branching, commit messages, code-quality gates, security & PII handling, and accessibility. Referenced by every other Defra plugin so cross-cutting standards live in one place. No agent — skills + hooks only. ([DEFRA/defra-ai-plugins](https://github.com/DEFRA/defra-ai-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/defra-shared))

- **deglaze**: Strip the sycophancy. Makes Claude honestly audit its own claimed-complete work against 17 named under-delivery patterns instead of polishing a summary over half-finished tasks. ([LuciferDono/deglaze](https://github.com/LuciferDono/deglaze))
- **dehumanize**: Makes AI work like AI — no human time estimates, no asking for accessible data, no emotional labor. ([88plug/dehumanize](https://github.com/88plug/dehumanize))
- **deja-vu**: Your agent starts already knowing what you did before — recall across Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, opencode and a dozen others. ([vshulcz/deja-vu](https://github.com/vshulcz/deja-vu/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin))
- **deja-vu**: Structured prior-art hunt that runs before anything custom gets built. ([vishnujayvel/deja-vu](https://github.com/vishnujayvel/deja-vu/tree/HEAD/codex-plugin))
- **dejared**: Java decompiler and JAR analyzer. Decompile classes, search bytecode, and read resources embedded in JAR, WAR, EAR, and AAR archives. ([hqkh4nh/dejared-mcp](https://github.com/hqkh4nh/dejared-mcp))

- **dejavu**: Has anyone done this before? ([Ravencloned/skills-for-claude](https://github.com/Ravencloned/skills-for-claude/tree/HEAD/dejavu))

- **dektools**: Optional DekSpec operator toolkit (ADR-047): project boards, code-quality/security review, brownfield onboarding, handoff, troubleshooting, issue-tracker glue. Write output to a file and read only the STATUS/VERDICT header to keep the main context clean. ([hkasahar/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/hkasahar/claude-code-skills))
